(function(){const st=load('dn-trade',{});
 const num=id=>parseFloat(($(id).value||'').replace(/,/g,''));
 ['#tdPrice','#tdFee','#tdWant','#dmN','#dmRate'].forEach(id=>{if(st[id]!=null&&st[id]!=='')$(id).value=st[id]});
 const rows=st.rows||[['',''],['',''],['',''],['','']];
 $('#upRows').innerHTML=rows.map((r,i)=>`<div class="uprow" data-i="${i}"><input placeholder="${['거래소','상점','친구','기타'][i]}" value="${esc(r[2]||'')}"><input type="number" min="0" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="가격" value="${r[0]||''}"><input type="number" min="1" inputmode="numeric" placeholder="수량" value="${r[1]||''}"></div>`).join('');
 function calc(){const o={};['#tdPrice','#tdFee','#tdWant','#dmN','#dmRate'].forEach(id=>o[id]=$(id).value);
  const p=num('#tdPrice'),f=num('#tdFee'),w=num('#tdWant');
  if(!isNaN(f)&&f>=0&&f<100&&(!isNaN(p)||!isNaN(w))){let h='';if(!isNaN(p)){const get=Math.floor(p*(1-f/100));h+=`${get.toLocaleString()}<small>수수료 ${Math.ceil(p*f/100).toLocaleString()} 빠지고 받는 금액</small>`}
   if(!isNaN(w)){const need=Math.ceil(w/(1-f/100));h+=`<div style="margin-top:6px">${need.toLocaleString()}<small>${w.toLocaleString()}을 받으려면 올릴 총액</small></div>`}$('#tdOut').innerHTML=h}
  else $('#tdOut').innerHTML='—<small>판매 금액과 수수료율을 넣어 주세요</small>';
  const R=[...document.querySelectorAll('#upRows .uprow')].map(r=>{const v=[...r.querySelectorAll('input')].map(x=>x.value);return {n:v[0]||r.querySelector('input').placeholder,p:parseFloat(v[1]),q:parseFloat(v[2]),el:r,raw:[v[1],v[2],v[0]]}});
  o.rows=R.map(r=>r.raw);save('dn-trade',o);
  const ok=R.filter(r=>r.p>0&&r.q>0);R.forEach(r=>r.el.classList.remove('best'));
  if(ok.length){ok.sort((a,b)=>a.p/a.q-b.p/b.q);ok[0].el.classList.add('best');$('#upOut').innerHTML=`${ok[0].n} 개당 ${(ok[0].p/ok[0].q).toLocaleString(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:2})}<small>${ok.map(r=>r.n+' '+(r.p/r.q).toLocaleString(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:2})).join(' · ')}</small>`}
  else $('#upOut').innerHTML='—<small>가격과 수량을 넣으면 개당 가격이 가장 싼 곳을 찾아줘요</small>';

let crc='전체',crg='전체';
function won(n){return n==null?'도안':n>=10000?(n%10000?(n/10000).toFixed(1).replace('.0',''):n/10000)+'만':n.toLocaleString()}
function crRender(){const cats=['전체','무기','방어구','장신구','유물·성물','재료·소모품'];
  $('#crChips').innerHTML=cats.map(c=>`<button type="button" class="${c===crc?'on':''}" data-c="${c}">${c} ${c==='전체'?CRAFT.length:CRAFT.filter(x=>c==='유물·성물'?(x.c==='유물'||x.c==='성물'):x.c===c).length}</button>`).join('');
  $('#crGr').innerHTML=['전체','노멀','레어','에픽','유니크'].map(g=>`<button type="button" class="${g===crg?'on':''}" data-g="${g}">${g==='전체'?'모든 등급':g}</button>`).join('');
  const q=($('#crQ').value||'').replace(/\s+/g,'');
  const L=CRAFT.filter(x=>(crc==='전체'||(crc==='유물·성물'?(x.c==='유물'||x.c==='성물'):x.c===crc))&&(crg==='전체'||x.g===crg)&&(!q||(x.n+x.m.map(m=>m[0]).join('')+(x.s||'')).replace(/\s+/g,'').includes(q)));
  $('#crCnt').textContent=L.length+'개 레시피';
  $('#crList').innerHTML=L.map(x=>`<div class="crc"><img loading="lazy" src="${IB}craft/${x.id}.webp" alt="${x.n}" width="130" height="160" data-zoom="1"><div><b class="${x.g?'g-'+x.g:''}">${x.n}</b><div class="sub">${x.s?x.c+' · '+x.s:x.c}${x.g?` <span class="gb g-${x.g}">${x.g}</span>`:''}</div><ul>${x.m.map(m=>`<li><span>${m[0]}</span><span>${m[1]==null?'':'×'+m[1]}</span></li>`).join('')}</ul></div><div class="ft"><span>성공 ${x.r[0]}%${x.r[1]?' · 대성공 '+x.r[1]+'%':''}</span><span>골드 ${won(x.gd)}</span>${x.x?`<span>경험치 ${x.x.toLocaleString()}</span>`:''}${x.gr?`<span style="color:#e9d9ae">대성공: ${x.gr}</span>`:''}<button type="button" class="crcb" data-id="${x.id}">💰 손익 계산</button></div></div>`).join('')||'<p class="mute">찾는 레시피가 없어요.</p>'}
$('#crChips').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;crc=b.dataset.c;crRender()});
$('#crGr').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('button');if(!b)return;crg=b.dataset.g;crRender()});
$('#crQ').addEventListener('input',crRender);crRender();
let CRP={m:{},s:{},n:1,fee:0};try{CRP=Object.assign(CRP,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('dn-crp')||'{}'))}catch(e){}
const crSave=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem('dn-crp',JSON.stringify(CRP))}catch(e){}};
const crG=n=>!isFinite(n)?'-':Math.abs(n)>=1e8?(Math.round(n/1e7)/10).toLocaleString()+'억':Math.abs(n)>=1e4?(Math.round(n/1e3)/10).toLocaleString()+'만':Math.round(n).toLocaleString();
function crCalc(id){const x=CRAFT.find(c=>c.id===id),el=$('#crCalc');if(!x){el.hidden=true;return}el.hidden=false;el.dataset.id=id;
  const bd={노멀:[1,1],레어:[1,1],에픽:[1,0],유니크:[0,0]}[x.g];const r0=x.r[0]||0,r1=x.r[1]||0;const sp=CRP.s[id]||['',''];

function crOut(){const el=$('#crCalc');const x=CRAFT.find(c=>c.id===el.dataset.id);if(!x)return;const N=Math.max(1,Math.min(9999,parseInt($('#crN').value)||1)),fee=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,+$('#crFee').value||0))/100;CRP.n=N;CRP.fee=fee*100;
  let mat=0;el.querySelectorAll('#crMat tbody tr').forEach(tr=>{const q=+tr.querySelector('.crq').dataset.q,inp=tr.querySelector('input'),p=Math.max(0,+inp.value||0);CRP.m[inp.dataset.m]=inp.value===''?undefined:p;tr.querySelector('.crq').textContent=(q*N).toLocaleString()+'개';tr.querySelector('.crs').textContent=crG(q*N*p);mat+=q*N*p});
  const bd={노멀:[1,1],레어:[1,1],에픽:[1,0],유니크:[0,0]}[x.g]||[0,0];const s0=Math.max(0,+(($('#crS0')||{}).value)||0),s1=Math.max(0,+(($('#crS1')||{}).value)||0);CRP.s[x.id]=[($('#crS0')||{}).value||'',($('#crS1')||{}).value||''];
  el.querySelectorAll('.cre').forEach(td=>td.textContent=(N*(+td.dataset.p)/100).toLocaleString(undefined,{maximumFractionDigits:2})+'개');
  const r0=(x.r[0]||0)/100,r1=(x.r[1]||0)/100,v0=s0*(bd[0]?1:1-fee),v1=s1*(bd[1]?1:1-fee),gd=(x.gd||0)*N,cost=mat+gd,inc=N*(r0*v0+r1*v1),prof=inc-cost;
  const c=(t,v,col)=>'<span>'+t+' <b'+(col?' style="color:'+col+'"':'')+'>'+v+'</b></span>';
  $('#crOut').innerHTML=c('재료비',crG(mat))+c('제작비',crG(gd))+c('총 원가',crG(cost),'#FFE812')+c('1개당 원가',crG(cost/N))+(s0||s1?c('기대 수입',crG(inc))+c('기대 순이익',(prof>=0?'+':'')+crG(prof),prof>=0?'#7de0a8':'#ff8f8f')+c('모두 일반 성공',(N*v0-cost>=0?'+':'')+crG(N*v0-cost))+(r1?c('모두 대성공',(N*v1-cost>=0?'+':'')+crG(N*v1-cost)):''):'<span class="mute">결과 값을 넣으면 순이익이 나와요</span>');crSave()}

function sRender(){$('#sysChips').innerHTML=['전체','성장','전투','서버'].map(c=>`<button type="button" class="${c===sf?'on':''}" data-c="${c}">${c}</button>`).join('');

const RM=[
 {w:'출시 초반',i:['뱀파이어 도시','인마대전','바알 순차 공개'],now:1},
 {w:'1~2개월 차',i:['헬가오 레이드','일일 던전 추가']},
 {w:'3개월 차 · 첫 대규모 업데이트',i:['타이탄 포함 신규 지역·에피소드','신규 클래스','야탄 신전 상층','공성전']},
 {w:'4~6개월 차',i:['라인하르트 공성전 「골렘 침공」','다른 차원 공성전 개입','신규 보스 마리로즈','새 무기 계열']}];
$('#homeRoad').innerHTML=RM.map(r=>`<div class="st ${r.now?'now':''}"><h3>${r.w}</h3><ul class="facts small" style="margin:0">${r.i.map(x=>`<li>${x}</li>`).join('')}</ul></div>`).join('');
$('#rmList').innerHTML=RM.map(r=>`<div class="card"><h3 style="color:var(--champ)">${r.w}${r.now?' <span class="bd new" style="position:static;vertical-align:middle;margin-left:6px">지금</span>':''}</h3>${r.i.map(x=>`<span class="pill">${x}</span>`).join('')}</div>`).join('');

/* 강화 기록기 */
let EN=load('dn-enh',[]);
const enDraft=load('dn-enh-in',{s:'',l:''});$('#enSlot').value=enDraft.s;$('#enLv').value=enDraft.l;
const enKeep=()=>save('dn-enh-in',{s:$('#enSlot').value,l:$('#enLv').value});
$('#enSlot').addEventListener('input',enKeep);$('#enLv').addEventListener('input',enKeep);
function enGroups(){const by={};for(const r of EN){const key=r.s+'|'+r.l;(by[key]||(by[key]={s:r.s,l:r.l,n:0,ok:0,f:0}));by[key].n++;by[key].ok+=r.r;by[key].f+=r.f}
  return Object.values(by).sort((a,b)=>a.s.localeCompare(b.s)||a.l-b.l)}
function enRender(){const rows=enGroups();const ok=EN.filter(r=>r.r).length,fab=EN.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.f,0);
  $('#enStat').innerHTML=`<div><small>전체 시도</small><b>${EN.length}</b></div><div><small>성공</small><b>${ok}</b></div><div><small>전체 성공률</small><b>${EN.length?Math.round(ok/EN.length*100)+'%':'-'}</b></div><div><small>실패로 얻은 파브라늄</small><b>${fab}</b></div>`;
  $('#enTable tbody').innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(x=>`<tr><td>${esc(x.s)} +${x.l} → +${x.l+1}</td><td class="n">${x.n}</td><td class="n">${x.ok}</td><td class="n">${Math.round(x.ok/x.n*100)}%</td><td class="n">${x.f}</td></tr>`).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" class="mute">아직 기록이 없어요. 위에서 첫 강화를 기록해 보세요.</td></tr>';
  $('#slotList').innerHTML=[...new Set(EN.map(r=>r.s))].map(s=>`<option value="${esc(s)}">`).join('');
  $('#cFrom').innerHTML='<option value="">직접 입력</option>'+rows.map(x=>`<option value="${(x.ok/x.n*100).toFixed(2)}">${esc(x.s)} +${x.l} → +${x.l+1} · ${Math.round(x.ok/x.n*100)}% (${x.n}회)</option>`).join('')}
$('#enForm').addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('button[data-r]');if(!b)return;
  const s=$('#enSlot').value.trim(),l=parseInt($('#enLv').value,10),f=Math.max(0,parseInt($('#enFab').value,10)||0),r=+b.dataset.r;
  if(!s||isNaN(l)){toast('부위와 단계를 입력하세요');return}
  EN.push({s,l,r,f:r?0:f,t:Date.now()});save('dn-enh',EN);
  if(r)$('#enLv').value=l+1;$('#enFab').value=0;enKeep();enRender();toast(r?'성공을 기록했어요':'실패를 기록했어요')});
$('#enUndo').onclick=()=>{if(!EN.length)return;EN.pop();save('dn-enh',EN);enRender();toast('마지막 기록을 지웠어요')};
$('#enCopy').onclick=()=>copy('부위,단계,결과,파브라늄,시각\n'+EN.map(r=>[r.s,r.l,r.r?'성공':'실패',r.f,new Date(r.t).toLocaleString('ko-KR')].join(',')).join('\n'),'기록을 복사했어요');
enRender();

/* 계산기 */
const cIn=['cP','cCost','cBack','cQ'];$('#cPre').addEventListener('change',e=>{if(!e.target.value)return;$('#cP').value=e.target.value;$('#cP').dispatchEvent(new Event('input',{bubbles:true}))});const cSaved=load('dn-calc',null);
if(cSaved)cIn.forEach(id=>{if(cSaved[id]!=null)$('#'+id).value=cSaved[id]});
const fmt=n=>isFinite(n)?(Math.round(n*10)/10).toLocaleString('ko-KR'):'-';
function cRender(){const p=Math.min(100,Math.max(0,+$('#cP').value))/100,cost=+$('#cCost').value||0,back=+$('#cBack').value||0,q=Math.min(99.9,Math.max(1,+$('#cQ').value))/100;
  const o={};cIn.forEach(id=>o[id]=$('#'+id).value);save('dn-calc',o);
  if(!(p>0)){$('#cAvg').textContent='-';$('#cStat').innerHTML='';$('#cBars').innerHTML='<p class="mute">성공률을 0보다 크게 넣어 주세요.</p>';return}
  const avg=1/p,net=avg*cost-(avg-1)*back,need=p>=1?1:Math.ceil(Math.log(1-q)/Math.log(1-p)),bad=p>=1?1:Math.ceil(Math.log(.1)/Math.log(1-p));
  $('#cAvg').textContent=fmt(avg)+'회';
  $('#cStat').innerHTML=`<div><small>평균 재료<br>(돌려받은 양 제외)</small><b>${fmt(net)}</b></div><div><small>목표 ${Math.round(q*1000)/10}%를 위해<br>준비할 횟수</small><b>${need.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}회</b></div><div><small>그만큼의<br>재료</small><b>${fmt(need*cost)}</b></div><div><small>운이 나쁠 때<br>(하위 10%)</small><b>${bad.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}회</b></div>`;
  const marks=[...new Set([1,2,3,5,Math.round(avg),need,Math.ceil(need*1.5)].filter(n=>n>=1))].sort((a,b)=>a-b).slice(0,8);
  $('#cBars').innerHTML=marks.map(n=>{const c=1-Math.pow(1-p,n);return `<div class="hb"><span>${n.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}회</span><span class="t"><i style="width:${c*100}%"></i></span><span>${Math.round(c*1000)/10}%</span></div>`}).join('')}
cIn.forEach(id=>$('#'+id).addEventListener('input',cRender));
$('#cFrom').addEventListener('change',e=>{if(e.target.value){$('#cP').value=e.target.value;cRender()}});
cRender();

let GH=load('dn-gh',[]);
function ghRender(){$('#ghList').innerHTML=GH.length?GH.map((g,i)=>`<div class="card"><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap"><h3 style="margin:0;color:var(--champ)">${esc(g.n)} <span class="pill" style="margin-left:6px">${esc(g.u)}</span></h3><div style="display:flex;gap:6px"><button type="button" class="ghost" data-cp="${i}">복사</button><button type="button" class="ghost" data-del="${i}">삭제</button></div></div>
  <div class="slots" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="slot a"><small>공격형</small>${esc(g.a||'—')}</div><div class="slot d"><small>방어형</small>${esc(g.d||'—')}</div><div class="slot s"><small>보조형</small>${esc(g.s||'—')}</div></div></div>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">저장된 프리셋이 없어요. 위에서 첫 조합을 저장해 보세요.</div>'}
$('#ghForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();const n=$('#ghName').value.trim();if(!n)return;
  GH.unshift({n,u:$('#ghUse').value,a:$('#ghA').value.trim(),d:$('#ghD').value.trim(),s:$('#ghS').value.trim()});save('dn-gh',GH);
  ['ghName','ghA','ghD','ghS'].forEach(id=>$('#'+id).value='');ghRender();toast('프리셋을 저장했어요')});
$('#ghList').addEventListener('click',e=>{const d=e.target.dataset.del,c=e.target.dataset.cp;
  if(d!=null){GH.splice(+d,1);save('dn-gh',GH);ghRender();toast('삭제했어요')}
  if(c!=null){const g=GH[+c];copy(`[${g.n} · ${g.u}] 공격형 ${g.a||'-'} / 방어형 ${g.d||'-'} / 보조형 ${g.s||'-'}`,'프리셋을 복사했어요')}});
ghRender();

/* 숙제 */
const HW_DEF=['런칭 14일 출석부 받기','일일 미션','히든 퀘스트 진행','골렘의 미궁 · 야탄 신전 사냥','필드 엘리트 「오염된 존재」 잡기','인마대전 참여','안 쓰는 장비 분해해서 파브라늄 모으기','+6 이상 안 쓰는 장비 왕실 무기고 납품','오버기어드 강화','차원 리그 공헌도 쌓기','비접속 모드 켜기 (Lv.30부터 하루 12시간)'];
const today=()=>new Date(Date.now()-5*3600e3).toLocaleDateString('ko-KR');
let HW=load('dn-hw',{d:today(),items:HW_DEF.map(t=>({t,c:false}))});
const hwDay=()=>{if(HW.d!==today()){HW.d=today();HW.items.forEach(i=>i.c=false);save('dn-hw',HW);return true}};
hwDay();
function hwRender(){const n=HW.items.length,c=HW.items.filter(i=>i.c).length;
  $('#hwBar').style.width=(n?c/n*100:0)+'%';$('#hwCnt').textContent=`${c} / ${n} 완료`;$('#hwDate').textContent=HW.d;
  $('#hwList').innerHTML=HW.items.map((i,k)=>`<div class="chk ${i.c?'done':''}"><input type="checkbox" id="hw${k}" data-k="${k}" ${i.c?'checked':''}><span><label for="hw${k}" style="font-weight:500;font-size:16px;margin:0;color:inherit">${esc(i.t)}</label></span><button type="button" class="ghost" data-del="${k}" aria-label="${esc(i.t)} 삭제">삭제</button></div>`).join('')||'<p class="mute">숙제가 없어요. 아래에서 추가하세요.</p>'}
$('#hwList').addEventListener('change',e=>{const k=e.target.dataset.k;if(k==null)return;HW.items[k].c=e.target.checked;save('dn-hw',HW);hwRender();if(HW.items.every(i=>i.c))toast('오늘 숙제 끝. 수고했어요')});
$('#hwList').addEventListener('click',e=>{const k=e.target.dataset.del;if(k==null)return;HW.items.splice(+k,1);save('dn-hw',HW);hwRender()});
$('#hwForm').addEventListener('submit',e=>{e.preventDefault();const t=$('#hwNew').value.trim();if(!t)return;HW.items.push({t,c:false});$('#hwNew').value='';save('dn-hw',HW);hwRender()});
$('#hwReset').onclick=()=>{HW={d:today(),items:HW_DEF.map(t=>({t,c:false}))};save('dn-hw',HW);hwRender();toast('기본 목록으로 되돌렸어요')};
setInterval(()=>{if(hwDay())hwRender()},60000);
hwRender();

const strip=s=>s.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'');
const IDX=Object.entries(PAGES).map(([k,p])=>({k,t:p.t,d:p.d,i:p.i,x:p.t+p.d}))
 .concat(CRAFT.map(x=>({k:'craft',t:x.n,d:(x.g?x.g+' ':'')+(x.s||x.c)+' 제작 · '+x.m.slice(0,2).map(m=>m[0]).join(', '),i:'i-anvil',x:x.n+x.m.map(m=>m[0]).join(' ')})))
 .concat(TITLES.map(t=>({k:'titles',t:'칭호 · '+t[0],d:t[4],i:'i-crown',x:t.join(' ')})))
 .concat(SYS.map(s=>({k:'systems',t:s.t,d:strip(s.f[0]),i:s.i,x:s.t+strip(s.f.join(' '))})))
 .concat(WEAPONS.map(w=>({k:'weapons',t:w.n,d:w.role,i:w.art,x:w.n+w.role+w.d+w.cls.map(c=>c.n+c.d).join(' ')})))
 .concat(RM.map(r=>({k:'roadmap',t:'로드맵 · '+r.w,d:r.i.join(', '),i:'i-map',x:r.w+r.i.join(' ')})));
let SJ=null,SJl=0;
const sLoad=()=>{if(SJ||SJl)return;SJl=1;fetch('/data/search.json').then(r=>r.ok?r.json():[]).then(j=>{SJ=j;if($('#sov').classList.contains('open'))sRun()}).catch(()=>{SJl=0})};
const sNorm=t=>t.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'');
const sSnip=(x,q)=>{const n=x.toLowerCase(),qq=q.toLowerCase();let i=n.indexOf(qq);if(i<0){const w=qq.split(/\s+/)[0];i=n.indexOf(w)}if(i<0)return x.slice(0,70)+(x.length>70?'…':'');const a=Math.max(0,i-28),b=Math.min(x.length,i+qq.length+50);return (a?'…':'')+x.slice(a,b)+(b<x.length?'…':'')};
function sRun(){const raw=$('#sq').value.trim(),q=sNorm(raw);
  if(!q){$('#sres').innerHTML=IDX.slice(0,6).map(r=>`<a href="#${r.k}">${med(r.i,'sm')}<span><b>${esc(r.t)}</b><small>${esc(r.d)}</small></span></a>`).join('');return}
  const res=IDX.filter(r=>sNorm(r.x).includes(q)).slice(0,6);
  let cards=[];if(SJ){cards=SJ.map(c=>{const tt=sNorm(c.t),xx=sNorm(c.x);return {c,sc:tt.includes(q)?2:(xx.includes(q)?1:0)}}).filter(o=>o.sc).sort((a,b)=>b.sc-a.sc).slice(0,12).map(o=>o.c)}
  const ph=(PAGES.home?'':''),ch=cards.map(c=>{const pg=c.k==='home'?'/':'/'+c.k+'/';return `<a class="sc" href="${pg}?c=${encodeURIComponent(c.t)}">${med((PAGES[c.k]&&PAGES[c.k].i)||'i-guide','sm')}<span><b>${esc(c.t)}</b><small>${esc((c.f?'자주 묻는 질문 · ':'')+((PAGES[c.k]&&PAGES[c.k].t)||c.p))} — ${esc(sSnip(c.x||c.t,raw))}</small></span></a>`}).join('');
  const top=res.map(r=>`<a href="#${r.k}">${med(r.i,'sm')}<span><b>${esc(r.t)}</b><small>${esc(r.d)}</small></span></a>`).join('');
  $('#sres').innerHTML=(top||ch)?top+(ch?'<p class="small mute" style="margin:8px 6px 2px">페이지 안 내용</p>'+ch:(SJ?'':'<p class="small mute" style="padding:6px 10px">페이지 안 내용 불러오는 중…</p>')):(SJ?'<p class="mute" style="padding:10px">찾는 내용이 없어요. <a href="#contact">제보</a>해 주시면 채울게요.</p>':'<p class="small mute" style="padding:10px">불러오는 중…</p>')}
const sOpen=e=>{if(e)e.preventDefault();$('#sov').classList.add('open');$('#sq').value='';sLoad();sRun();setTimeout(()=>$('#sq').focus(),30)};
const sClose=()=>$('#sov').classList.remove('open');
$('#sbtn').onclick=sOpen;$('#hsBtn').onclick=sOpen;
$('#sq').addEventListener('input',sRun);
$('#sov').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='sov'||e.target.closest('.sres a'))sClose()});
/* 긴 페이지 위 바로가기 · 검색 결과 카드로 이동 */
function tocAdd(){document.querySelectorAll('section.view[data-v]').forEach(sec=>{if(sec.dataset.v==='home'||sec.querySelector('.toc'))return;
  const cards=[...sec.querySelectorAll('.card')].filter(c=>!c.classList.contains('faq')&&!c.closest('.cdm')&&c.querySelector('h3,h2'));if(cards.length<5)return;
  const nav=document.createElement('nav');nav.className='toc chips';nav.setAttribute('aria-label','이 페이지 바로가기');
  nav.innerHTML='<span class="tl">바로가기</span>'+cards.slice(0,16).map(c=>{const t=c.querySelector('h3,h2').textContent.trim();return `<button type="button" data-t="${esc(t)}">${esc(t.length>20?t.slice(0,19)+'…':t)}</button>`}).join('');
  const at=sec.querySelector('.lead')||sec.querySelector('.ph');if(at)at.after(nav)})}
const cardBy=(sec,t)=>[...(sec||document).querySelectorAll('.card h3,.card h2')].find(h=>h.textContent.trim()===t);
const cardGo=h=>{if(!h)return;const c=h.closest('.faq')?h:h.closest('.card');c.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});c.classList.remove('flash');void c.offsetWidth;c.classList.add('flash')};
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const b=e.target.closest('.toc button');if(!b)return;cardGo(cardBy(b.closest('section'),b.dataset.t))});
setTimeout(()=>{tocAdd();const c=new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('c');if(c)setTimeout(()=>cardGo(cardBy(document.querySelector('section.view.on')||document.querySelector('section.view[data-v="'+(window.START||'home')+'"]'),c)),250)},700);
addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')sClose();if(e.key==='/'&&!/INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA/.test(document.activeElement.tagName)){sOpen(e)}});

/* 3D 첫 화면: 금빛 코어 + 떠다니는 크리스털 + 빛 입자 */
function initScene(){
  if(!window.THREE)return;
  const cv=$('#scene');let W=0,H=0,run=true,raf=0;
  const still=matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  let rd;try{rd=new THREE.WebGLRenderer({canvas:cv,antialias:true,alpha:true,powerPreference:'high-performance'})}catch(e){return}
  rd.setPixelRatio(Math.min(1.75,devicePixelRatio||1));rd.outputEncoding=THREE.sRGBEncoding;
  const sc=new THREE.Scene();sc.fog=new THREE.FogExp2(0x06142b,.055);
  const cam=new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(38,1,.1,100);cam.position.set(0,0,14);
  sc.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x2a4a80,.55));
  const k1=new THREE.PointLight(0xffd98a,3.2,30);k1.position.set(4,3,8);sc.add(k1);
  const k2=new THREE.PointLight(0x5fd3ff,4,40);k2.position.set(-6,-2,-4);sc.add(k2);
  const k3=new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x9fd8ff,.8);k3.position.set(-3,6,5);sc.add(k3);
  const root=new THREE.Group();sc.add(root);
  /* 코어 */
  const gold=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0xe8c378,metalness:1,roughness:.22,emissive:0x3a2400,emissiveIntensity:.35});
  const core=new THREE.Group();root.add(core);
  const gem=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1.15,0),new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x9fe4ff,metalness:.3,roughness:.08,emissive:0x1d6fa8,emissiveIntensity:.9,transparent:true,opacity:.92}));
  gem.scale.set(1,1.45,1);core.add(gem);
  const rings=[];[[2.1,.045,0],[2.65,.03,1],[3.3,.022,2]].forEach(([r,t,i])=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(r,t,12,160),gold);m.rotation.x=Math.PI/2+(i-1)*.45;m.rotation.y=i*.6;core.add(m);rings.push(m)});
  const cage=new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.EdgesGeometry(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(1.75,0)),new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0xe3c27a,transparent:true,opacity:.55}));core.add(cage);
  const halo=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:glowTex('rgba(120,210,255,.9)','rgba(40,120,220,0)'),blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,transparent:true}));halo.scale.set(9,9,1);core.add(halo);
  const halo2=new THREE.Sprite(new THREE.SpriteMaterial({map:glowTex('rgba(255,220,150,.7)','rgba(255,180,80,0)'),blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending,depthWrite:false,transparent:true}));halo2.scale.set(4.2,4.2,1);core.add(halo2);
  /* 크리스털 */
  const shardMat=new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({color:0x0b1a33,metalness:.85,roughness:.28,emissive:0x061226,flatShading:true});
  const edgeMat=new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x5fd3ff,transparent:true,opacity:.35});
  const shards=[];const N=innerWidth<700?12:20;
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++){const g=Math.random()<.5?new THREE.OctahedronGeometry(1,0):new THREE.TetrahedronGeometry(1,0);
    const m=new THREE.Mesh(g,shardMat);m.add(new THREE.LineSegments(new THREE.EdgesGeometry(g),edgeMat));
    const s=.25+Math.random()*.75;m.scale.set(s,s*(1+Math.random()*.9),s);
    const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2,r=4+Math.random()*6,cx0=innerWidth>820?3.6:0;m.position.set(cx0+Math.cos(a)*r*(Math.cos(a)<0&&innerWidth>820?.55:1),(Math.random()-.5)*8,-2-Math.random()*7+Math.sin(a)*2);
    m.userData={rx:(Math.random()-.5)*.01,ry:(Math.random()-.5)*.012,fy:Math.random()*Math.PI*2,base:m.position.y};root.add(m);shards.push(m)}
  /* 빛 입자 */
  const PC=innerWidth<700?260:600,pos=new Float32Array(PC*3),col=new Float32Array(PC*3);
  for(let i=0;i<PC;i++){pos[i*3]=(Math.random()-.5)*30;pos[i*3+1]=(Math.random()-.5)*18;pos[i*3+2]=(Math.random()-.5)*16;const c=Math.random()<.55?[1,.85,.55]:[.45,.85,1];col.set(c,i*3)}
  const pg=new THREE.BufferGeometry();pg.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));pg.setAttribute('color',new THREE.BufferAttribute(col,3));
  const pts=new THREE.Points(pg,new THREE.PointsMaterial({size:.09,map:glowTex('rgba(255,255,255,1)','rgba(255,255,255,0)'),vertexColors:true,transparent:true,depthWrite:false,blending:THREE.AdditiveBlending}));sc.add(pts);
  function glowTex(a,b){const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=128;const x=c.getContext('2d'),g=x.createRadialGradient(64,64,0,64,64,64);g.addColorStop(0,a);g.addColorStop(.35,a.replace(/[\d.]+\)$/,'0.35)'));g.addColorStop(1,b);x.fillStyle=g;x.fillRect(0,0,128,128);const t=new THREE.CanvasTexture(c);return t}
  let mx=0,my=0;addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{mx=e.clientX/innerWidth-.5;my=e.clientY/innerHeight-.5},{passive:true});
  function size(){const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();W=r.width;H=r.height;if(!W||!H)return;rd.setSize(W,H,false);cam.aspect=W/H;cam.updateProjectionMatrix();
    const wide=W>820;core.position.set(wide?3.6:0,wide?.2:-4.1,0);core.scale.setScalar(wide?1:.62);rd.render(sc,cam)}
  new ResizeObserver(size).observe(cv);size();
  const t0=performance.now();
  function frame(now){const t=(now-t0)/1000;
    core.rotation.y=t*.25;gem.rotation.y=-t*.6;cage.rotation.x=t*.15;cage.rotation.z=t*.1;
    rings.forEach((r,i)=>{r.rotation.z=t*(.25+i*.12)*(i%2?-1:1)});
    halo.material.opacity=.75+Math.sin(t*1.6)*.15;
    shards.forEach(s=>{s.rotation.x+=s.userData.rx;s.rotation.y+=s.userData.ry;s.position.y=s.userData.base+Math.sin(t*.5+s.userData.fy)*.35});
    const p=pg.attributes.position.array;for(let i=0;i<PC;i++){p[i*3+1]+=.006+(i%5)*.002;if(p[i*3+1]>9)p[i*3+1]=-9}pg.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
    root.rotation.y+=(mx*.25-root.rotation.y)*.04;root.rotation.x+=(my*.12-root.rotation.x)*.04;
    rd.render(sc,cam);if(run&&!still)raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame)}
  if(still)frame(t0+4000);else raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame);
  window.__scene=on=>{if(on&&!run){run=true;if(!still)raf=requestAnimationFrame(frame)}else if(!on){run=false;cancelAnimationFrame(raf)}};
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>window.__scene(!document.hidden&&!PAGES[location.hash.slice(1)]&&!PAGES[window.START]));
}

<script>
/* 방문자 집계 + 운영자 전용 표시 (오늘·누적). 방문자는 브라우저당 하루 1번만 셈. 운영자는 ?k=코드 로 한 번 열면 이 브라우저에서만 보임 */
(function(){
  var H='f4c7e457288081b49b546c3494dd03f9d876743bb83ac57dd3ad7e9b90a143c0', LS='tn_owner', API='https://hitscounter.dev/api/hit?url=', BASE='https://tempalnote.com/__stat/';
  function kst(){return new Date(Date.now()+9*3600e3).toISOString().slice(0,10)}
  function g(k){try{return localStorage.getItem(k)}catch(e){return null}} function s(k,v){try{localStorage.setItem(k,v)}catch(e){}}
  async function sha(x){var b=await crypto.subtle.digest('SHA-256',new TextEncoder().encode(x));return [...new Uint8Array(b)].map(v=>v.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('')}
  async function hit(key){var r=await fetch(API+encodeURIComponent(BASE+key),{cache:'no-store'});var x=await r.text();var m=[...x.matchAll(/(\d[\d,]*)\s*\/\s*(\d[\d,]*)/g)];if(!m.length)throw 0;return +m[0][2].replace(/,/g,'')}
  async function unlock(){var u=new URL(location.href),k=u.searchParams.get('k');if(!k)return;u.searchParams.delete('k');history.replaceState(null,'',u.pathname+(u.search||'')+u.hash);
    if(k==='off'){try{localStorage.removeItem(LS)}catch(e){}return} try{if(await sha('tn:'+k)===H)s(LS,'1')}catch(e){}}
  function owner(){return g(LS)==='1'}
  var FS='https://firestore.googleapis.com/v1/projects/tempalnote/databases/(default)/documents',DOC='projects/tempalnote/databases/(default)/documents',KEY='AIzaSyCDIjNyGscJBDEGk2r6dCKgC8d2xFibeiA';
  function kday(o){return Math.floor((Date.now()+9*3600e3)/864e5)-(o||0)}
  function pkey(){var h=(location.hash||'').slice(1);if(/^[a-z]{2,12}$/.test(h)&&window.PAGES&&PAGES[h])return h;var p=location.pathname.replace(/^\/+|\/+$/g,'').split('/')[0];return /^[a-z]{2,12}$/.test(p)?p:'home'}
  function commit(w){return fetch(FS+':commit?key='+KEY,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({writes:w}),keepalive:true}).catch(function(){})}
  var lastPv='';function pv(){var k=pkey();if(k===lastPv)return;lastPv=k;commit([{transform:{document:DOC+'/pv/'+kday(),fieldTransforms:[{fieldPath:k,increment:{integerValue:'1'}}]}}])}
  var SID='';try{SID=sessionStorage.getItem('tn_sid')||''}catch(e){}if(!/^[a-z0-9]{8,24}$/.test(SID)){SID=(Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)).replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'').slice(0,20);try{sessionStorage.setItem('tn_sid',SID)}catch(e){}}
  var beats=0;function beat(){if(document.hidden||beats>40)return;beats++;commit([{update:{name:DOC+'/on/'+SID,fields:{}},updateTransforms:[{fieldPath:'t',setToServerValue:'REQUEST_TIME'}]}])}
  function nowCount(){return fetch(FS+':runAggregationQuery?key='+KEY,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({structuredAggregationQuery:{structuredQuery:{from:[{collectionId:'on'}],where:{fieldFilter:{field:{fieldPath:'t'},op:'GREATER_THAN',value:{timestampValue:new Date(Date.now()-150e3).toISOString()}}}},aggregations:[{alias:'n',count:{}}]}})}).then(function(r){return r.json()}).then(function(j){return +((j[0]&&j[0].result&&j[0].result.aggregateFields&&j[0].result.aggregateFields.n&&j[0].result.aggregateFields.n.integerValue)||0)})}
  function pvGet(o){return fetch(FS+'/pv/'+kday(o)+'?key='+KEY).then(function(r){return r.ok?r.json():{}}).then(function(j){var f=j.fields||{},m={};for(var k in f)m[k]=+(f[k].integerValue||0);return m}).catch(function(){return {}})}
  window.__pvGet=pvGet;
  function count(){var d=kst(),pd=g('tn_vd');if(pd===d)return;s('tn_vd',d);hit('day-'+d).catch(function(){});hit('total').catch(function(){});commit([{transform:{document:DOC+'/pv/'+kday(),fieldTransforms:[{fieldPath:pd?'xret':'xnew',increment:{integerValue:'1'}}]}}])}
  async function draw(){
    var el=document.getElementById('ownerStat');
    if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ownerStat';el.style.cssText='position:fixed;left:12px;bottom:12px;z-index:9999;background:rgba(8,16,34,.95);border:1px solid #c9a24a;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 14px;font:600 13px/1.55 system-ui,sans-serif;color:#f3e7c4;box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.45);cursor:pointer';el.title='누르면 새로고침';el.onclick=draw;document.body.appendChild(el)}
    var d=kst(),rd=JSON.parse(g('tn_or')||'{}');
    try{var t=await hit('day-'+d),a=await hit('total');rd[d]=(rd[d]||0)+1;rd.all=(rd.all||0)+1;s('tn_or',JSON.stringify(rd));
      var today=Math.max(0,t-rd[d]),all=Math.max(0,a-rd.all);
      var nw=await nowCount().catch(function(){return null}),P=await pvGet(0),Y=await pvGet(1),tot=0,top=Object.keys(P).filter(function(k){return k==='home'||(window.PAGES&&PAGES[k])}).sort(function(a,b){return P[b]-P[a]});top.forEach(function(k){tot+=P[k]});
      el.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;color:#c9a24a;letter-spacing:.06em">운영자만 보임 · '+d.slice(5).replace('-','/')+'</div>'+(nw!=null?'지금 <b style="font-size:18px;color:#7de0a8">'+nw.toLocaleString()+'</b>명 보는 중<br>':'')+'오늘 <b style="font-size:18px">'+today.toLocaleString()+'</b>명 · 누적 <b style="font-size:18px">'+all.toLocaleString()+'</b>명'+((P.xret||P.xnew)?'<br><span style="font-size:12px">다시 온 사람 <b style="color:#7de0a8">'+(P.xret||0).toLocaleString()+'</b> · 처음 온 사람 '+(P.xnew||0).toLocaleString()+(Y&&(Y.xret||Y.xnew)?' <span style="color:#9aa">(어제 '+(Y.xret||0)+' · '+(Y.xnew||0)+')</span>':'')+'</span>':'')+(tot?'<div style="margin-top:6px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.5;color:#cfd8e6;max-width:240px">오늘 페이지뷰 '+tot.toLocaleString()+'<br>'+top.slice(0,8).map(function(k){return (k==='home'?'첫 화면':(window.PAGES&&PAGES[k]&&PAGES[k].t)||k)+' '+P[k]}).join(' · ')+'</div>':'');
    }catch(e){el.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:11px;color:#c9a24a">운영자만 보임</div>집계 서버 응답 없음 · 눌러서 다시'}
  }
  unlock().then(function(){ if(owner()){draw();setInterval(draw,60000)} else { count(); pv(); beat(); setInterval(beat,60000); addEventListener('hashchange',pv); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(!document.hidden)beat()}) } });
})();
</script>
